# Campaign 13 v2 — rows a solo worker can execute

**Landing marker:** Batch NNNN. **Date:** 2026-09-12.
**Authority:** companion to [`CAMPAIGN_13_V2_CLOUD_QUALITY_2026-09-12.md`](CAMPAIGN_13_V2_CLOUD_QUALITY_2026-09-12.md). It is **not** a status authority: the row-level authority stays [`QUEUE_2026-07-23_CAMPAIGN13.md`](QUEUE_2026-07-23_CAMPAIGN13.md) §1 and the campaign-level authority stays [`CAMPAIGN_STATE.md`](CAMPAIGN_STATE.md). This file classifies rows; it changes no row's disposition and mints no acceptance.
**The maintainer's ask, 2026-09-12, in one sentence:** which Campaign 13 v2 rows are defined fully enough, and safe enough, to hand to a **solo Astra or a solo Gemini 3.8 worker** with no Opus lead standing over the work.
**Measurement base:** every figure below was taken at HEAD `9f3723b0b3` (Batch 1481) unless the line says otherwise. Figures carried in from another agent's pass are attributed to it.

## 1. The solo-safety rubric

A row is **SOLO-NOW** only if **all ten** hold.

- **S1 — class.** Tools, docs, fixtures, data or spec repair. **No engine-semantic change** under `packages/engine/Source` (Renderer, Shaders, Scene logic). A docstring-only edit in an engine file is allowed **only** when the exact replacement text is given in the row.
- **S2 — machine-checkable acceptance.** A command plus its expected output. No "measure and decide", no visual judgement.
- **S3 — every value supplied.** Nothing is left as "the row decides and records why". No `[unverified]` input on the critical path.
- **S4 — sole ownership.** Every file the row touches is named and owned by nobody else. Live lanes at dispatch: **L1 Durin** (harness readiness, probe runtime, `C13-42b`), **L2 Telchar** (fleet governance, runner homes, quarantine runner, fleet-contract remediation), **L6 Yavanna** (cloud instruments, fixtures, HDR rule, scenes), **L8 Eönwë** (queue ID table, gate rewrite, N34 API sweep, wave-end gate + `capture-and-diff` served base), **Oromë** (WebGPU offscreen pick-depth readback, sample-height, typings preflight for sandcastle-smoke). Deferred engine lanes: **L3 Ulmo** (`C13-N10` resolver), **L4 Manwë** (N21/N20/N11 WGSL), **L5 Ossë** (N22 weather field), **L7 Tulkas** (N06 cost). A `package.json` edit is allowed only as **one add-only script-line entry whose exact text is given**.
- **S5 — premises verified at the tree at dispatch time**, and quoted in the row.
- **S6 — proof needs no browser and no gulp build** (Gemini), or is a fully scripted browser run with the exact command (Astra only).
- **S7 — size S or M**, one packet, one sitting. Gemini: under ~45 minutes of wall time with no long idle waits — two Gemini runs died on transport errors on 2026-09-12.
- **S8 — STOP conditions enumerated:** a premise disagrees with the tree; a spec goes red for a reason outside the row; a file outside the list must change; a value is missing. Stop and report; never improvise.
- **S9 — forbidden actions enumerated:** git writes; builds; rewriting any file over 100 lines; inventing values; touching `migration_doc/archive`; moving or repointing files; editing a file a live lane owns.
- **S10 — review is unchanged.** Landing still goes through the seat's station-3 review and landing gates. "Solo" means no lead **during** the work, not no review.

**Classes.** **SOLO-NOW** — all ten hold. **SOLO-AFTER-TEMPLATE** — all ten hold once an Opus lead has done a first instance that can be copied. **NOT-SOLO** — state which S-item fails.

### 1.1 Evidence-based additions, from the two workers' measured records

Gemini:

1. Every column of every row the lane writes is pre-filled by the brief, or the brief names the exact placeholder (`—`) and the note that explains it; the brief carries a column-by-column source table per deliverable. *Basis: `REVIEW_CALMACIL.md` F1/F5 — 74 rows of `Pri`/`Class` and nine `Parity: both` invented where the plan defined no such column.*
2. Each row states which words are **byte-verbatim from `<file>:<lines>`** and which are the worker's own prose. *Gemini's verbatim work measured 12/12 rulings and 65/65 rows exact; its free-text judgement produced F6 and F7.*
3. A documentation correction names its **rendered** form: strike or rewrite the false clause in visible text, then the dated marker. An HTML comment is never the whole correction; a patch whose only change at a correction site is a comment is a review blocker. *Basis: F2.*
4. Every filename the lane writes resolves to a tracked path or to a file landing in the same patch. Before freeze the lane greps its own patch for the scratchpad directory name and the untracked source filenames and reports the counts (expected 0). *Basis: F3 (twelve `Basis:` lines), F4 (two never-landed companions).*
5. **No background waits.** No long-running background commands, no idle "waiting for the task to complete" turns; every command runs in the foreground with its exit code read immediately. *Basis: both 2026-09-12 transport deaths occurred while "root agent idle; waiting for … background task(s)", with eight consecutive pause lines before the error.*
6. Cap one `agy` call at **30 minutes** and size the brief to fit. Longest successful run measured 35 min 51 s; split anything larger into two briefs with a checkpoint file between them.
7. **One transport failure ends the Gemini lane.** The brief names the ~~Sonnet~~ **Astra** fallback worker and its handover-header brief path before dispatch. *[corrected 2026-09-13, R-2026-09-13-8: Sonnet is not a solo worker; the named fallback is Astra. One death still ends the lane.]* <!-- corrected 2026-09-13, R-2026-09-13-8 --> *Basis: the seat's own lesson — "fall back to Sonnet after ONE transport failure, not two"; Ulbar (Sonnet) delivered all seven fixes in ~45 minutes after the two deaths.*
8. The brief enumerates the files the lane may touch; the reviewer diffs the patch path set against that list, and any rename, id change or edit in a file the lane was only meant to **cite** is an automatic FIX. *Basis: the Saeros lane renamed a tracked ledger id (`DEFERRED_WORK.md:16406`).*
9. **Gemini amends, it does not rewrite.** Where a section rewrite is authorised, the brief lists the facts that must survive (quoted from `git show HEAD:<file>`) and the reviewer diffs old against new specifically for dropped facts. *Basis: F6.*
10. In any ledger row, observation and interpretation are **separate sentences**; a brief that narrows a symptom says "keep the measurement, add the interpretation". *Basis: F7.*
11. **Budget the fix round** rather than treating it as an exception: five of the last seven Gemini lanes returned LAND-WITH-FIXES (Salgant 12, Thror 15, Ciryandil/Tar-Meneldur 8; only Girion landed clean). Schedule reviewer + fix round + confirm for every Gemini batch, per `R-2026-09-10-4`'s mandatory station-3 review.
12. The packet reports any gate that is **vacuous** over the touched paths, with the count of files actually checked, not just the exit code. *Basis: `LANDING_PACKET_CIRYANDIL.md:35` did this correctly for prettier; `.markdownlintignore` and `.prettierignore` make lint vacuous for `migration_doc`.*

Astra:

13. **Astra never works in the seat.** One named clone per lane, stated in the brief and in the packet; `F:/Dev/GH/cesium-webgpu` is read-only to it. *Basis: `R-2026-09-10-3`; `ORCHESTRATION_HANDBOOK.md:76-80`; `WORKER_ISOLATION_AND_BRANCH_HANDOFF.md:689-693`; `AGENTS.md:148-152`.*
14. **One row per clone in the lane ledger, opened at dispatch:** clone path, base commit, patch path, freeze md5, packet path, reviewer, verdict. A clone with no row is not a lane. *Basis: the 2026-09-06…09 window produced four work groups and no such row for any of them.*
15. **Freeze before anything else:** every reviewable unit becomes `<lane>.patch` (`git diff HEAD --binary --no-renames`) plus a FREEZE md5 and a packet the same day, per unit — never a dirty tree with a narrative. *Basis: `AGENTS.md:153-157`; `RETURN_AUDIT` F1.*
16. *(both)* A **superseded freeze file is deleted or renamed** when a fix round regenerates the patch; a stale `FREEZE_*.md5` beside a current one is a landing hazard. *Basis: `REVIEW_CALMACIL.md` fix-round residual (a).*
17. Any change to shared runtime, a coordination mechanism, or a required field is staged **optional-with-default**, and the lane runs the full contract and fleet runners before freeze and reports their counts. A newly required descriptor field is a fleet-wide breaking change. *Basis: `RETURN_AUDIT` F3; `DEFERRED_WORK.md:76-84`, `:114-118`.*
18. A behavioural narrowing that reaches past the named feature, and any default flip, are **separate hunks, separately reviewed**; a default flip additionally needs a non-refused capture at ≥1280×720 plus a frame-cost number before it is proposed. *Basis: `RETURN_AUDIT` F2, F16; `R-2026-09-10-6`.*
19. Every spec gets its **npm runner home in the same batch**, and the packet quotes `node Tools/spec-runner-census.mjs` for the specs it adds. *Basis: `RETURN_AUDIT` F10; `R-2026-08-29-1`.*
20. An apparatus that **refuses every run is a ruling request filed the same session**, with source review continuing in parallel; a blocked capture is never re-run as a work queue. *Basis: `AGENTS.md:158-162`; `RETURN_AUDIT` F13 (six runs, `verdicts: []`).*
21. **Standing maintainer holds are re-read at lane start** and named in the packet; any document move or deletion under the 2026-06-30 archival hold is an automatic block, and the lane gates `verify-readme-index.mjs` with the change staged. *Basis: `AGENTS.md:163-165`; `RETURN_AUDIT` F5, F19.*
22. **No dependency mutation outside an owned clone and none in a served clone**; every install command is captured with its exit code, and manifest, lock and installed tree are reconciled in one reviewed batch. *Basis: `AGENTS.md:171-175`; `RETURN_AUDIT` F4.*
23. *(both)* **Placeholder discipline at landing:** the stamp step greps the **whole patch** for the placeholder token, never a named-file list. *Basis: the 2026-09-12 seat miss — eleven `Executed:` lines still carrying the unstamped batch placeholder survived Batch 1476 and needed Batch 1477. (This sentence deliberately does not reproduce the placeholder token, so the only literal occurrence in this file is the one at :3 that the seat stamps.)*

## 2. The two worker profiles

### 2.1 Gemini 3.8 Flash High (via `agy`, tier 3)

**Can.** Bulk structured-row placement at speed with byte fidelity — Tar-Meneldur ran 09:40:38 → 10:16:29 (35 min 51 s) for "73 full rows added in `DEFERRED_WORK.md` (lines 542-1706); 74 rows added in `QUEUE_2026-07-23_CAMPAIGN13.md` §1 ID table (lines 156-229)", 13 files / +2,747 / −51. Exhaustive set-identity and verbatim work that holds under an adversarial read — `REVIEW_CALMACIL.md` §6: "65/65 ids set-identical across three files, 56/56 structured fields exact, 65/65 row prose traceable to its own plan row, 12/12 rulings and 2/2 notes byte-verbatim". Re-measures rather than copying a stale figure (same review §4, the four bucket counts independently re-derived). Writes honest packets — "No claim in either packet was found overstated"; `LANDING_PACKET_CIRYANDIL.md:35` volunteers "prettier checks zero files in `migration_doc/` and is not claimed". Honours explicit out-of-class boundaries and brief traps. **Zero git writes across every observed run** — all six `gemini-*.log` files end "git-write check: HEAD/branches/stash unchanged". Runs the Node doc gates itself and reports exit codes unprompted. Fast on a bounded record round: Thror wrote 7 files / +214 / −11 in 11 min 6 s and applied a 15-item fix round in 8 min 31 s. Can land clean first pass when the deliverable is small and fully specified (Girion's `R-2026-09-11-1` batch → "Bard LAND", Batch 1460).

**Cannot.** Leave a field empty when the source supplies none — it fills it in (F1, F5). Pick a correction form a reader will actually see (F2). Keep its own citations coherent with what it lands in the same patch (F3, F4). Judge which facts of a rewritten section are load-bearing (F6). Separate a measurement from its interpretation (F7). Stay in scope inside a file it was only meant to cite (the Saeros ledger-id rename). Calibrate a claim's scope. Survive a long unattended `agy` run. Engine, shader or layout judgement, station-3 review, or any Edge/browser leg — excluded by directive and by `R-2026-09-10-4`.

**Limits.** Successful wall clocks measured: 10 min 26 s, 35 min 51 s, 11 min 6 s, 8 min 31 s. Transport fragility: two deaths on 2026-09-12, both "subscriber fell behind updates, stalled for 5s" (78 s and 11 min 27 s), **both while idling on a background task**; idle-wait lines measured 20 of 52, 13 of 47 and 10 of 29 log lines. Dispatch only through the `agy` CLI wrapper as a foreground Bash call, never as an Agent (the tracked rule is [`ORCHESTRATION_HANDBOOK.md`](ORCHESTRATION_HANDBOOK.md):72 and [`WORKER_ISOLATION_AND_BRANCH_HANDOFF.md`](WORKER_ISOLATION_AND_BRANCH_HANDOFF.md):686; the invocation script itself lives in the seat’s untracked scratch and is deliberately not a tracked path); its review is still an Opus Agent. Git: read-only plus `git add -N`; the wrapper asserts HEAD/branches/stash unchanged and exits 4 otherwise. No browser, server, `npm install` or gulp build. CLI shape is brittle (`--flag=value`; `GEMINI.md` is not auto-loaded in print mode, hence the wrapper's prepended rules). Its markdown gates are vacuous where it works most. Mandatory independent Opus station-3 review on every deliverable.

**Observed failures.** F1 (74 invented `Pri`/`Class` cells, plus guessed P1s for three ids that `git grep` finds nowhere), F5 (nine invented `Parity: both`), F2 (five corrections as non-rendering HTML comments, two fact-inverting sentences left standing), F3 (twelve `Basis:` lines citing an untracked scratchpad filename), F4 (a landed plan citing two documents that never land), F6 (two campaign-level facts silently dropped in an authorised rewrite), F7 (a banked measurement deleted rather than re-interpreted). Overall verdict on the 58/59 docs landing: LAND-WITH-FIXES, eight findings, fixes confirmed only after a different worker's fix round. Two `agy` transport deaths on that fix round produced **zero** edits. Plus the Saeros out-of-scope ledger-id rename, the Mithrellas markdown-hygiene/overstatement defects, and the Lalaith weak-heuristic inventory. Seat's grade to the maintainer: **Gemini B+**.

### 2.2 Astra (in a named clone, never the seat)

**Can.** Deep, multi-day engine work of real architectural value that was later adopted into HEAD — the eight-function prepared-frame cloud lifecycle ("Fix applied (adopted from Astra's C13 work)", `WEBGPU_DEBUGGING_LOG.md:21159`) and the probe runtime/lifecycle adopted as `C13-42a` (`DEFERRED_WORK.md:69`). Produce landing records that verify literally — `RETURN_AUDIT` F20: all five source byte-counts and SHA-256s match, 10 of 11 named artifacts verify, `git ls-files | wc -l = 8,823` as claimed, independent verdict EVIDENCE GO. Commits that pass every governed rule unaided (landing-guard EXIT=0; landing-rules + pre-push-guard 110/110). Disciplined recovery of shared state (the 2026-09-08 `.git/index` truncation, repaired with dry run, approval, byte-verified backups and a packet). Green Node gates on its own engine work. Finds real defects by reading source rather than by apparatus. Files ruling requests and keeps red evidence red where it does escalate.

**Cannot.** Be trusted in the seat worktree (`R-2026-09-10-3`, transplant + seat restoration to clean Batch 1450). Convert work into reviewable units on its own cadence — three days produced 72 modified / 34 deleted / 146 untracked with an empty index and nothing committed after 2026-09-06 19:50. Contain a change to its stated scope (F16, F2). Change shared runtime without breaking the fleet (F3: a required `descriptor.workBudgetMs` turned a green CI-registered spec into 0/5 pass). Give its specs a runner home (F10: 232 spec files with runner NONE). Respect a standing maintainer hold (the 34-document archive move under the 2026-06-30 hold, leaving `verify-readme-index` exit 1 with 50 violations). Change a machine-wide coordination mechanism acceptably (its Edge-slot rewrite was NOT adopted). Escalate a blocked apparatus instead of working around it (six 2026-09-09 runs, all `outcome: refused`, `verdicts: []`). Mutate dependencies safely outside an owned clone. Edit governance under its own authority.

**Limits.** Unsupervised window 2026-09-06 00:33 → 2026-09-09 03:22; output in it: engine 12 tracked files +3,443/−1,948, tooling 48 files +16,609/−2,055, `CAMPAIGN_STATE.md` +539, `QUEUE_2026-07-23_CAMPAIGN13.md` +337 with rows C13-42…C13-49 opened and zero closed, ~29 branch/`ASTRA_*.md` files, 146 untracked paths. Git: capable of commits and of passing the pre-push guard, but its two commits sat unpushed from 2026-09-06 until the seat pushed them on 09-10. Browser: refuses or declines captures. Build: builds successfully **and bakes dirt into the served bytes**. Disk: `tmp/` 4.7 GB, 4.3 GB of it Astra's. Visual acceptance is owed, not delivered. Naming/hygiene defects now permanent in history. Held to this day: `R-2026-09-10-5` (archive sweep) and `R-2026-09-10-6` (the `Globe.js` cloud default-on hunk).

**Observed failures.** Seat-worktree occupation for three days ending in an index truncation and a forced transplant; F1 (a whole wave existing only as uncommitted worktree state); F2 (volumetric clouds flipped ON by default for every WebGPU scene at tier-3 "high" with no frame-time measurement, and the default-on appearance is itself the reported defect); F3; F4 (a declared Sharp override no machine ever resolved, `npm audit --json = 4 high`); F7 (its uncommitted probe rewrite colliding head-on with an in-flight lane patch); F10; F15 (three engine entry points left caller-less, unrecorded); F16; F21/F22. Its slot rewrite rejected at adoption; its probe-runtime rewrite measured as a fleet-wide breaking change (21 probes, 0 declaring the new required field) and re-staged as opt-in on one predicate; six banked runs executed against an unlanded, untracked tree. Seat's grade to the maintainer: **Astra B−**.

## 3. SOLO-NOW rows, in full

*[2026-09-13, R-2026-09-13-8: the **worker assignments** in this section are superseded by §9, which re-cuts the roster to two solo profiles — nineteen Astra rows and two Gemini rows. Every "Sonnet" below is unassigned; Sonnet is not a solo worker. The **row definitions** — the eleven fields, the edits, the anchors and the acceptance of each row — are NOT superseded and remain this file's authority. `DX-84` moves from SOLO-AFTER-TEMPLATE to SOLO-NOW (Astra) and `C13-N58` from Gemini to Astra; both keep the text they have here.]* <!-- corrected 2026-09-13, R-2026-09-13-8 -->

Twelve rows — **eleven SOLO-NOW plus `DX-84`, which is SOLO-AFTER-TEMPLATE per R-HANDOFF-10 (§4) and is specified here in full because its template is already in hand.** Thirteen were drafted: `C13-N08b-C` was demoted to NOT-SOLO by seat ruling **R-HANDOFF-8** (2026-09-13), so §3.11 is now a pointer and its failing item is in §5. **New ids allocated by this pass** (add-only, continuing the tracked sequences — highest existing `C13-N` was `C13-N57`, highest existing `DX` was `DX-81`): `C13-N58`, `C13-N59`, `DX-82`, `DX-83`, `DX-84`. The seven `C13-N08b-*` sub-rows are **sub-ids of the already-tracked `C13-N08b`**, filed on the `C13-42a-2` precedent; no id anywhere is renumbered.

Every row below carries the same eleven fields. Where a premise cites `file:line`, those lines were re-read at `9f3723b0b3`. Where a row's earlier draft mis-cited a line, the correction is stated in the row rather than silently applied.

### 3.1 `C13-N08b-A1` — repair `cloud-coverage-response.spec.mjs` A1: the three coverage gates moved to a hoisted threshold local

**Worker:** Sonnet — was Gemini; **R-HANDOFF-9** (2026-09-13) adjudicates §6.3(3) and re-assigns every Gemini-assigned spec repair to Sonnet, and **R-HANDOFF-4** reaches the same value from the worker reader's assignment. **Class/size:** spec repair · S. **Estimate:** 35 min. **Runner home:** existing, no `package.json` edit — `npm run test-cloud-c13-quarantine` (`package.json:208`, landed Batch 1479). Moving the spec out of quarantine into `test-cloud-c13` (`:207`) is a seat script-line union at landing, not this row's work.
**Owned files:** `Tools/visual-regression/cloud-coverage-response.spec.mjs`.

**Verified premises.** `:144` `const gates = [...cloudSource.matchAll(/smoothstep\(\s*1\.0 - ([^,]+),/g)];`; `:145` asserts `gates.length, 3`; `:146-148` assert each argument is `cloudEffectiveCoverage(effectiveCoverage)`; `:150-152` are the IBL leg (leave byte-identical); `:156` `assert.doesNotMatch(cloudSource, /smoothstep\(1\.0 - effectiveCoverage/);`. Production: `ProceduralClouds.wgsl:1286-1287` `let coverageThreshold = 1.0 - cloudEffectiveCoverage(effectiveCoverage);` / `density = smoothstep(coverageThreshold, 1.0, density);`, identical pairs at `:1371-1372` and `:1442-1443` — measured counts: `let coverageThreshold = 1.0 - ` = 3, `smoothstep(coverageThreshold, 1.0, density)` = 3, `smoothstep(1.0 - ` = **0**. The hoist is `b8bbb990df` (Batch 1108). `:50` `const read = (relative) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, relative), "utf8");` — **no CRLF normalisation**, so every anchor written here must be single-line. Baseline: `node --test` → 11 / 10 / 1, exit 1; `npm run test-cloud-c13-quarantine` → 150 / 135 / 15.

**Deliverable.** A1 goes green **without weakening it**, by matching the hoisted-threshold spelling the engine has used since Batch 1108 and by re-pointing the now-vacuous negative guard at the defect's modern shape. Two preconditions, both also stop conditions: the clone's `git status --porcelain` is **empty** at the base commit, and every acceptance command runs with cwd = the clone root. Three edits inside the test that begins at `:143`; **execute them bottom-up and anchor each by its exact line text**, because edit (2) inserts ~9 lines. (1) Replace `  assert.doesNotMatch(cloudSource, /smoothstep\(1\.0 - effectiveCoverage/);` with `  assert.doesNotMatch(cloudSource, /let coverageThreshold = 1\.0 - effectiveCoverage;/);` — the old spelling occurs 0 times, so the guard as written is vacuous. (2) Immediately **before** `  const iblGates = [...iblSource.matchAll(/smoothstep\(1\.0 - ([^,]+),/g)];`, separated by one blank line, insert a consumption check: a two-line comment ("The hoisted local must still be what the density gate consumes, or the / response curve is computed and thrown away.") then `assert.equal((cloudSource.match(/smoothstep\(coverageThreshold, 1\.0, density\)/g) || []).length, 3, "each hoisted threshold must feed its own density gate");` prettier-wrapped. (3) Replace `  const gates = [...cloudSource.matchAll(/smoothstep\(\s*1\.0 - ([^,]+),/g)];` with `  const gates = [...cloudSource.matchAll(/let coverageThreshold = 1\.0 - ([^;]+);/g)];`. Leave `:145`, `:146-148`, the IBL leg and its `doesNotMatch` byte-identical.

**Acceptance.** (1) `git status --porcelain` **first**, from the clone root → empty; any output means a dirty base and the row STOPS. (2) `node --test Tools/visual-regression/cloud-coverage-response.spec.mjs` → 11 / 11 / 0, exit 0 (base 11/10/1). (3) `git diff --stat -- <the spec>` → exactly 1 file changed, and `git diff --stat` with no pathspec shows that same single file. (4) `node <lane-tmp>/inertness-a1.mjs` **from the clone root** — it reads the WGSL, replaces every `cloudEffectiveCoverage(effectiveCoverage)` with `effectiveCoverage`, then prints `argsOk` (every hoisted threshold's argument still matches) and `guardOk` (the raw-coverage spelling still absent) → **`argsOk false guardOk false`**; either printing `true` means the repair is inert and the row STOPS. An `ENOENT` is a wrong-cwd error, not a finding. (5) `npm run test-cloud-c13-quarantine` → still exit 1, totals 150 / 136 / 14, no `cloud-coverage-response` failure. **R-HANDOFF-6 (2026-09-13):** those totals assume this row lands before its siblings; measure the quarantine baseline in your own clone at base, and if it differs because a sibling repair already landed, record **both** numbers and continue — a sibling's success is not a stop, and the seat reconciles the arithmetic at landing.

**Stop.** Non-empty `git status --porcelain` at base; any premise not reproducing (in particular a `let coverageThreshold = 1.0 - ` count other than 3, or a first run other than 11/10/1); the inertness script printing `true`; a file outside the owned spec needing to change; the test going green only by deleting or loosening an assertion.
**Forbidden.** Any edit under `packages/engine/`; `package.json` or any npm script; the other six quarantined specs; git writes (`git add -N` for patch production only); gulp build, `npm install`, browser, dev server; rewriting the spec file (>100 lines — targeted edits only); executing the three edits top-down or addressing any by line number; writing a mutant as a string literal carrying leading whitespace; `migration_doc/`.
**Packet.** `_lane-out/LANDING_PACKET_<Name>.md` (base sha, the clean-base status capture, five acceptance commands with verbatim stdout tails, the diff stat, an explicit "no engine file and no `package.json` changed", and the measured prettier/markdownlint file count for this path), `<lane>.patch`, `FREEZE_<NAME>.md5`, `log-cloud-coverage-response.txt`, `log-inertness-a1.txt`, `log-quarantine.txt`.

### 3.2 `C13-N08b-A2` — repair `cloud-ibl-revision.spec.mjs` B1: the refresh predicate and the revision commit now live in two named functions

**Worker:** Sonnet — was `either`; **R-HANDOFF-3** (2026-09-13) sends every `either` row to Sonnet unless it is docs-only, and this one is spec-code repair. **Class/size:** spec repair · M. **Estimate:** 50 min. **Runner home:** existing — `npm run test-cloud-c13-quarantine` (`package.json:208`).
**Owned files:** `Tools/visual-regression/cloud-ibl-revision.spec.mjs`.

**Deliverable.** A sweep operator can ask, in one command with no browser and no server, whether this machine is carrying browser processes whose launcher has died, and how much memory they hold. **This tool reports. It never kills** — no kill flag, no `--force`, no `taskkill`, no `process.kill`. Deciding to end someone else's process is a human call and belongs to a separate, maintainer-approved row. **(1)** `Tools/lib/browser-orphan-preflight.mjs`, whose file **must** begin with the full JSDoc block given in the brief (summary line; a `@purpose` sentence naming parent-dead `msedge`/`chrome`/`chromium`/`firefox`/`webkit`/`playwright` roots, their descendant trees, the memory each holds and the machine's free memory, and ending "Reports only; it never kills a process."; `@status ACTIVE`; then a `WHY POWERSHELL` paragraph — a parent pid is not reachable from node alone on Windows, and Principle 12 prefers node and allows a shell where strictly required). It exports three things. `parseProcessCsv(text)`: CRLF-normalises, drops blank lines, throws `unexpected header: <line>` unless the first line is byte-exactly the four-column header, throws `unparsable row: <line>` unless each remaining line matches `/^"(\d+)","(\d+)","([^"]*)","(\d+)"$/`, returns `{pid, ppid, name, rss}` with all four numeric and `name` raw. `findOrphanRoots(rows)`: a row is a **root** when `name` matches `/^(msedge|chrome|chromium|firefox|playwright|webkit)\.exe$/i` **and** its `ppid` is not the `pid` of any row in the same listing; descendants are walked transitively with a cycle-safe `seen` Set; returns `[{root, tree, bytes}]` sorted by `bytes` descending then `root.pid` ascending. A parent-dead non-browser is not a root; a browser whose parent is alive is not a root even if its grandparent is dead — **the root test is one level, the tree walk is transitive**. `PROCESS_QUERY`: the frozen `{command, args}` pair measured above. **CLI when it is the entry point:** non-`win32` → print `browser-orphan-preflight: unsupported platform <platform>; this preflight reads Win32_Process` and exit 2; otherwise `execFileSync` with `encoding:"utf8"`, `maxBuffer: 64*1024*1024`, `timeout: 60000`, and on any throw print `browser-orphan-preflight: process query failed: <message>` and exit 2. On success print, in order: `browser-orphan-preflight: <N> processes, <M> parent-dead browser root(s)`; one line per root (omitted when M is 0) matching **exactly** `/^ {2}pid \d+ \S+ parent \d+ \(dead\) tree \d+ proc \d+\.\d{2} GiB$/`; `memory: free <f> GiB of <t> GiB` from `os.freemem()`/`os.totalmem()`; then `PASS: no parent-dead browser processes` or `FAIL: <M> parent-dead browser root(s)`. `process.exitCode` 0 when M is 0, 1 otherwise — **0 PASS / 1 FAIL / 2 ERROR**, the first three tiers of the frozen table; there is no STRUCTURAL case here, do not invent one. **(2)** `Tools/lib/browser-orphan-preflight.spec.mjs`, a pure `node --test` spec that **never** spawns PowerShell and never reads the live process table, beginning with the two-line `// @purpose …` / `// @status ACTIVE` header, using a 9-row CRLF-joined fixture (System 4; explorer 1000; msedge 18776←19296 with children 12624 and 21140; msedge 2200←1000 with child 2201; chrome 3300←33001; conhost 4400←44001) and **exactly twelve** `test(...)` calls in this order: T1 nine rows; T2 exactly two roots; T3 entry 0 is pid 18776 / ppid 19296 / `msedge.exe`; T4 entry 0 has `tree.length` 3 and `bytes` **153391104**; T5 entry 1 is pid 3300 / ppid 33001 / `chrome.exe` / tree 1 / bytes 7000000; T6 bytes-descending; T7 pid 2200 in no root and no tree; T8 pid 4400 in no root; T9 `parseProcessCsv('"a","b"')` throws with that exact message; T10 the bad-row throw; T11 `PROCESS_QUERY.args[3]` contains `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process` and `Object.isFrozen(PROCESS_QUERY)`; **T12 the inertness mutant**, in the shape of `lane-tmp.spec.mjs:309-342` — take a host directory with `mkLaneTmp("browser-orphan-mutant-", {laneName:"browser-orphan-spec-mutant"})`, read this module's own source, prefix the root filter's condition so it reads `false && …` in a **copy**, assert the copy differs, write it into the host, `await import(pathToFileURL(file).href)`, assert the mutant returns **0** roots over the same fixture, and remove the host in a `finally`. **(3)** `package.json`: append exactly ` Tools/lib/browser-orphan-preflight.spec.mjs` (one leading space) to the **end** of the `test-tools-lib` value at `:170`. Remove nothing, reorder nothing, create no new script, touch no other line — it is pinned `text eol=lf`, so an editor that writes CRLF into it produces a whole-file diff and is an automatic stop. **Landing hazard to name in the packet, not to solve:** the `:167`/`:170` hunk adjacency above.

Measured: clean → writes `[{8,24},{48,16}]`, unowned 0; mutant → `[{8,24},{8,56}]`, unowned 1. **And:** the mutant leg **alone** does not pin the widening (unowned is 1 with or without `ranges.emitter`); the clean-module leg is what fails when EDIT 2 is absent, which is why E6 has two legs. **No engine file is edited** — every mutant is a source-text rewrite performed by `bundle({mutate})` at test time.

**Acceptance.** (1) `node --test <spec>` → before 19 / 16 / 3 with failures B4, C2, C3; after **20 / 20 / 0**, exit 0. (2) `node --test <spec> 2>&1 | grep -c "^ok "` → 20 — **this holds only when the output is piped** (non-TTY TAP); under a bare TTY the spec reporter is used and the count is 0, which is not a failure. (3) `grep -c "E6: with updateConfig's second write re-aimed across the frustum, B4 fails" <spec>` (double-quote the pattern; the name contains an apostrophe) → 1. (4) `grep -c "occlusionFarCutoff" <spec>` → **0** after (6 before — three OLD/NEW injection pairs). (5) `grep -n "owns: \[ranges" <spec>` → exactly three lines at **110 / 115 / 120**, shifted +1 from 109/114/119 because EDIT 1 adds a line. (6) `grep -c "^test(" <spec>` → 20 (19 before). (7) **Revert only EDIT 2 in a scratch copy**, re-run, restore → `not ok 10 - B4` **and** `not ok 20 - E6`, 20 / 18 / 2, exit 1. Do this on a copy in your lane temp root, never by editing the tracked file twice. (8) `npx prettier --check <spec>` → exit 0 (measured byte-identical, 21,542 B in / out, CRLF preserved). (9) `npx eslint <spec>` → exit 0. (10) `npm run test-cloud-c13` → before 540 / 537 / 3 with the only three failures `not ok 403 - B4`, `not ok 406 - C2`, `not ok 407 - C3`; after 541 / 541 / 0 — **derived by arithmetic over the two measured runs, not separately measured; report the actual numbers you observe**. Run this leg **only on a built clone**. (11) `grep -c "godray-sun-usability-uniform-ranges.spec.mjs" package.json` → 1, unchanged. (12) `git status --porcelain` on the spec → ` M <spec>`, and the total porcelain count exactly one higher than at lane start.

**Stop.** Any of the six OLD blocks not found byte-exactly (**exception for EDIT 5**: a 0-match there is more likely your tooling processing the literal `\n` at `:552-553` than a moved file — re-check with a byte-exact editor and the 550-558 range before declaring a mismatch); a baseline other than 19/16/3 with those three names; any test other than B4/C2/C3 red before the edit, or any test other than the named ones red after it; E6 passing on a copy with EDIT 2 reverted, or failing with it applied (**do not land an inert control**); prettier or eslint reporting anything (do not hand-reformat); any path other than the owned spec in `git status --porcelain`; **concluding the engine is wrong and wants a code change** — it is not, and `DEFERRED_WORK.md:1660-1664` asserts the opposite in tracked text, which is the seat's to correct, not yours; one `agy` transport failure (hand over to the Sonnet fallback, do not retry).
**Forbidden.** Any git write beyond read-only git plus `git diff HEAD --binary --no-renames`; `packages/engine/**`, including `WebGPUGodRayEffect.ts`, which this row cites at seven places and must not touch; `package.json`; `migration_doc/**` (the lanes that held these files have landed and their clones are deleted, so the restriction now stands on one reason only: **the ledger row, the three "19 tests / 5 mutants" lines and the `TOOLING_CATALOG` census are the seat's to write at landing** — disclose them, do not edit them); `migration_doc/archive/**`; rewriting the 568-line file; relaxing, deleting or skipping any assertion instead of re-anchoring the mutation; applying any edit through a heredoc, `node -e`, `sed` or `awk`; running the catalog generator (it reads the committed catalog from the git **index**, so a lane regeneration cannot clear `--check` anyway); `npx gulp build`, browser, dev server, `npm install`, and any backgrounded or long-running command; adding, renaming or removing any test other than the single new E6, or renumbering E1-E5; temp files outside `<os.tmpdir()>/cesium-lane/<yourname>/`.
**Packet.** `_lane-out/<LANE>.patch` (expected one file, **+57/−44** — the earlier +48/−44 estimate predated E6's two-leg form), `_lane-out/FREEZE_<LANE>.md5` (patch md5 and base sha, two lines) and `_lane-out/LANDING_PACKET_<LANE>.md` containing, in order: base sha and clone path; the six edits with a one-line confirmation each that the OLD text matched exactly once, and for EDIT 5 whether the exact-text anchor or the 550-558 fallback was used; BEFORE and AFTER `node --test` counts verbatim; the **EDIT-2-reverted** run's counts showing E6 red; every acceptance command with its measured output and exit code; the **two E6 rationale paragraphs verbatim**; and four required disclosures — (a) C3 carries no inertness control at HEAD and this row adds none (E5's mutant leaves C3 green either way; C2's control is E5, B4's is the new E6), (b) prettier and eslint **do** cover this path and both pass while the markdown gates are vacuous because no markdown is touched, (c) the four doc-drift sites this row's 19→20 / 5→6 change invalidates (`FEATURE_INVENTORY.md:792`, `DEFERRED_WORK.md:2033`, `CAMPAIGN_13_V2_CLOUD_QUALITY_2026-09-12.md:365`, `TOOLING_CATALOG.md:599`), (d) the `DEFERRED_WORK.md:1660-1664` ledger line that wrongly calls B4 a real contract violation; and the sentence "No engine file was edited; every mutant is a bundler source-text rewrite."

## 6. The critic pass — findings incorporated, and findings left open

A second agent re-measured this classification at the same HEAD with the seat left unmodified. Its findings are recorded here in full; **"incorporated" means the row text in §3–§5 already carries the correction, "OPEN" means the seat adjudicates.**

### 6.1 Missing rows it found

1. **`NEW-CLOUD-UNIFORM-MIRROR-DENSITY-DOMAIN-PROBE` — OPEN, and fold it into L3's batch.** A sixth stale `CLOUD_UNIFORM_FLOATS` mirror nobody listed: `Tools/visual-regression/probe-cloud-density-domain.mjs:990` reads `evidence.uniformFloatCount === 168` while the value it mirrors is **172** (`WebGPUProceduralCloudRenderer.ts:180-182`). *Independently re-verified by this pass at the tree.* The pin is a conjunct of every lane's `passed` (`:977-995`) which is a conjunct of `artifactValid` (`:1093`), so the probe cannot report valid at HEAD; it dates from Batch 743 (`47a940eed9`) and survived because the probe needs a browser. **Not a standalone solo row** — writing 172 hours before Ulmo writes 176 is guaranteed rework.

Two stale campaign-level statements with replacements on the record: `:248-252` ("job 13b owed") and `:103-104` (item 8 written as pending while `DEFERRED_WORK.md:180` reads **Item 8 CLOSED 2026-09-12**). Demonstrated on the batch that caused the lesson (`3a9d6b80f0`, Batch 1471): its 6 non-spec sources → 7 specs → runners including **`test-cloud-c13`**, exactly the runner that went red unnoticed. S4 clean, no `package.json` edit (the census spec is homed in `test-landing-rules` at `:168`).
4. **`NEW-SPEC-HOME-RATCHET` — OPEN, SOLO-NOW after one line of seat assent (S10).** *Re-measured by this pass:* `node Tools/spec-runner-census.mjs --json` → `{"totalSpecs":375,"homed":166,"quarantined":7,"orphaned":209}`. `spec-census` (`package.json:211`) is referenced by no aggregate runner and no CI guard, and the census spec drives synthetic fixtures only, so nothing stops orphan 210. Characterization-only, the shape spec group G already established.

The seat already ordered a path-set diff for L5-after-L3 on the shared renderer; the same diff is owed for L3-vs-L4 on this spec.

### 6.2 Rubric weaknesses it found

1. **INCORPORATED (addition 3, and §3.9's acceptance).** S2 is satisfiable by an acceptance that never touches the defect — Gemini F2 landed five corrections as non-rendering HTML comments. Every docs-correction row now carries a **must-return-zero** grep for the false clause beside the must-match grep.
2. **OPEN — extend S3.** S3 governs **inputs** only; both workers' worst failures are **outputs** (F1's 74 invented `Pri` cells, F5's nine `Parity: both`). Proposed wording: "every field of every artifact the row writes is pre-filled, or the row names the exact placeholder and the note that explains it."
3. **OPEN — no S-item covers blast radius.** Astra F3 reddened the fleet with one required field; Batch 1471 reddened `test-cloud-c13` and nobody ran it. Proposed: the row lists the runners `node Tools/spec-runner-census.mjs --files <touched paths> --json` reports, with expected pass/fail per runner — measurable today (§6.1 item 3).
4. **OPEN — S4's `package.json` clause is wrong-grained.** Measured: three live clones each carry a 1-insertion/1-deletion edit on three **different** keys (manwe `test-cloud-c13`, orome `test-sandcastle`, osse `test-visual-regression-node`); ulmo has none. The landing machinery already resolves this per key. So the real constraint is per-**key** contention, and "one add-only script-line entry" also mis-describes the shape in the tree (extending a runner's path list is a 1/1 modification, not an add). At least one demotion and two survivor caveats rest on the per-file reading.
5. **OPEN, and consequential — S4 is being evaluated against a roster instead of the tree.** L1, L2, L6 and L8 all **landed** (Batches 1478-1481) and their clones are archived; *this pass confirms* the only live clones are `cesium-lane-{manwe,orome,osse,ulmo}-20260912` (plus `before-ar001`, `sync-1145` and this one). Every S4 rejection of the form "L1 owns the probe runtime" is therefore void, which re-opens at least `C13-42a-3` items 2/4/6/7. **S4 must be discharged by `git status --porcelain` over the clones that exist at dispatch**, and S5's "verified at the tree at dispatch time" must cover ownership, not only `file:line`.
6. **OPEN — nothing sizes a row for the fix round the evidence predicts**, and the worker column contradicts the seat's own standing rule: five of seven recent Gemini lanes returned LAND-WITH-FIXES, and the rule is "short bounded **docs** tasks", yet five of the thirteen survivors assign Gemini to `.spec.mjs` repair with regex mutants — tools **code**. Proposed: an S-item in which the row's class (docs prose / tools code / measurement) selects the worker independently of size.
7. **OPEN — nothing requires the solo lane's output to become a frozen, reviewable artifact.** Astra F1 is that failure in pure form, and a solo lane is exactly where it recurs. Proposed: move addition 15 ("`<lane>.patch` + FREEZE md5 + packet, the same sitting") into the rubric proper.
8. **OPEN — S6 is a property of the machine, not of the row.** Measured: `webgpu-cloud-godray-current-mask-order` 24/24 and `cloud-primary-ray` 5/5 pass at the seat **because** the generated `Shaders/WebGPU/Environment/*.js` modules are present here; on a fresh clone they are gitignored (`packages/engine/.gitignore:5`) and the same specs are 0/24 and 1/4. S6 must read "no browser and no build **in the worker's clone**", and any row touching a spec that imports a generated shader module must declare that dependency. *(§3.6 already does.)*

The clause added is the reverse: the row must forbid the new index row or doc header from citing a scratchpad path and must point every evidence reference at the worker-archive path (85 tracked `migration_doc` lines already cite it, and `verify-tracked-references.mjs` does not police markdown citations).

## 7. Dispatching a solo worker

1. **Clone.** From `F:/Dev/GH`: `git clone --no-hardlinks F:/Dev/GH/cesium-webgpu F:/Dev/GH/cesium-lane-<name>-<yyyymmdd>`, then from the seat `node Tools/provision-worker-clone.mjs <clone>` (exit 0). Record `git log -1 --format='%h %s'`. Run `git status --short` in the clone and record the provisioner-modified paths — today that is `migration_doc/MAINTAINER_RULINGS_2026-08-17.md`, CRLF-only with an empty `git diff --numstat`; **exclude them from the lane's patch**. `DX-82` (§3.8) exists to remove this step.
2. **Open the lane's ledger row at dispatch** (addition 14): clone path, base commit, patch path, freeze md5, packet path, reviewer, verdict. A clone with no row is not a lane.
3. **Brief.** The row's eleven fields **are** the brief. Add the worker-specific additions from §1.1 that apply, name the ~~Sonnet~~ **Astra** fallback and its handover brief path before dispatch (addition 7; fallback corrected 2026-09-13, R-2026-09-13-8) <!-- corrected 2026-09-13, R-2026-09-13-8 -->, and state which words are byte-verbatim and which are the worker's own (addition 2).
4. **Gemini.** Dispatch **only** through the `agy` CLI wrapper as a **foreground** Bash call with the brief as `--print` input — never as an Agent ([`ORCHESTRATION_HANDBOOK.md`](ORCHESTRATION_HANDBOOK.md):72, [`WORKER_ISOLATION_AND_BRANCH_HANDOFF.md`](WORKER_ISOLATION_AND_BRANCH_HANDOFF.md):686, `R-2026-09-10-4` at [`MAINTAINER_RULINGS_2026-09-10.md`](MAINTAINER_RULINGS_2026-09-10.md):48). The wrapper script is the seat’s own untracked scratch file, not a tracked path, so it is named by role rather than cited by filename. Flags are Go-style `--flag=value`. `GEMINI.md` is not auto-loaded in print mode, which is why the wrapper prepends the rules; the wrapper also asserts HEAD/branches/stash unchanged and exits 4 otherwise. Cap the call at 30 minutes. **One transport failure ends the lane** — hand the row unchanged to the Sonnet fallback.
5. **Astra.** One named clone per lane, stated in the brief and in the packet. `F:/Dev/GH/cesium-webgpu` is read-only to it; it never works in the seat.
6. **Freeze, the same sitting.** `git diff HEAD --binary --no-renames` (with the provisioner paths excluded) → `_lane-out/<lane>.patch`; its md5 → `_lane-out/FREEZE_<LANE>.md5`; the packet → `_lane-out/LANDING_PACKET_<LANE>.md`. A fix round that regenerates the patch **deletes or renames the superseded FREEZE file** (addition 16).
7. **Review and land.** An independent Opus station-3 reviewer stands behind every deliverable (`R-2026-09-10-4`), and the reviewer diffs the patch path set against the row's `ownedFiles` (addition 8). Budget the fix round (addition 11). **The seat reviews and lands** — the lane never commits, never pushes, and never merges.
8. **Close out.** Repatriate any visual evidence into the main repo's gitignored `Tools/visual-regression/output/` before the clone is reset or deleted, then sweep the lane temp root (`Tools/lib/lane-tmp.mjs`, `Tools/temp-hygiene.mjs`).

## 8. Handoff — the dispatch pack for these rows (added 2026-09-13)

Everything a solo worker needs that no row can carry for itself — the dispatch procedure, the rubric and its twenty-three additions restated for a stranger, the three worker profiles, the roster with its ruled worker assignments and the sequencing between the rows — is [`SOLO_WORKER_HANDOFF_2026-09-13.md`](SOLO_WORKER_HANDOFF_2026-09-13.md), which lands with this file.
That page carries seat rulings **R-HANDOFF-1 … R-HANDOFF-11** (2026-09-13), which adjudicate open items of §6.2 and §6.3: the four Gemini-assigned spec repairs re-assigned to Sonnet (§6.3(3)), `C13-N08b-C` demoted to NOT-SOLO (§6.3(2)), `DX-84` confirmed SOLO-AFTER-TEMPLATE with Sonnet (§6.3(4)), the three `either` rows resolved, and the quarantine-baseline contradiction — which is stated in that page's §5.1, not in this file — turned into a rule.
Where that page and this file disagree about **who** works a row or **when**, that page governs — it is dated later. Where they disagree about a row's **wording**, this file governs: it is the row-text authority and that page is the procedure.

## 9. Re-cut 2026-09-13 under R-2026-09-13-8: two solo profiles

*Added 2026-09-13 by the re-cut assembler lane (**Everard**, Opus) at `0e4b898129` (Batch 1486), seat Gandalf. **A note on the number:** the re-cut brief called this section "§7", but `§7` (Dispatching a solo worker) and `§8` (Handoff) were already in use at the tree, so it is **appended as §9 rather than renumbering anything** — add-only, per this file's own rule and Principle 11. Everything that cites "companion §7" for the re-cut means this section.*

*Inputs: the four group reads, the four classify passes, the three refutations that ran, and the critic pass (Estella, `critic.json`), **whose `finalTable` is authoritative** over the classify and refute files wherever they disagree. The critic measured at the same `0e4b898129`. Where a row below contradicts §3, §4 or §5 above about **who** works it, this section governs; where they disagree about a row's **wording**, §3 still governs — it remains the row-text authority.*

### 9.1 The ruling, verbatim

> # Maintainer rulings — 2026-09-13
>
> ## R-2026-09-13-8 — The solo roster is re-cut to two workers: Astra alone in a named clone takes everything fully defined; Gemini takes the bounded remainder
>
> R-8 the solo roster: there are TWO solo workers. **Astra** runs alone as its own worker, reviewer and tester in a named clone — never the seat — and may take EVERYTHING FULLY DEFINED: tools, docs, fixtures AND engine/shader rows, running the full proof bar itself (a behaviour spec, inertness mutants that change the output, its own station-3 checklist on its own patch, and the named Edge leg under the machine's Edge-slot lock in `Tools/visual-regression/lib/probe-edge-slot.mjs`). Excluded from Astra only: files a live seat lane holds, rows whose design shape is undecided, and seat-only mechanics (landing, rulings). **Gemini** takes the rows that are overkill for Astra: bounded docs and mechanical work under thirty minutes, every value specified, no build, no browser. **"Sonnet" is NOT a solo worker.** `R-HANDOFF-3`, `R-HANDOFF-7` and `R-HANDOFF-10` are superseded to the extent they assign a row to Sonnet; their non-worker substance stands. The seat still reviews at landing.
>
> Basis: maintainer direction of 2026-09-13, given after the wave-1 landings (Batches 1478-1481) and the P0-2 fix (Batch 1483) left twelve dispatchable rows assigned to a worker class the maintainer has withdrawn; supersedes the roster of `SOLO_WORKER_HANDOFF_2026-09-13.md` §4 and the worker column of `CAMPAIGN_13_V2_SOLO_ROWS_2026-09-12.md` §3. Astra's authority is widened from `R-2026-09-10-3` (never the seat worktree) and from the profile's browser exclusion, both of which this ruling overrides for a named clone under the slot lock; charter §4.6's author-may-not-approve rule is displaced for solo lanes by the self-review bar the seat writes into the dispatch page, and the seat's landing review remains the independent check.
>
> Executed: <BATCH-PLACEHOLDER>.
>
> Authority: charter §1.1.

Its home file is `migration_doc/MAINTAINER_RULINGS_2026-09-13.md (new; add it to migration_doc/README.md in the same batch or verify-readme-index goes red)`. **Two bookkeeping riders the seat owes with it** (problem P-09 below): no `R-2026-09-13-1`…`-7` exists anywhere at the tree, so the seat either mints the day's other rulings or records in one sentence why the sequence starts at `-8`; and the `Executed:` placeholder is stamped by grepping the **whole patch**, never a named-file list — eleven unstamped `Executed:` lines survived Batch 1476 exactly that way.

### 9.2 The criteria this pass applied

**Astra, A1–A6.** The four classify passes used one six-point test, consistently but never written out in a numbered list in any input; it is reconstructed here from its use across all four passes and from the ruling's own words, and is the assembler's wording, not a quotation:

| # | Criterion | How it is discharged |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **A1** | **Fully defined.** Every file, every edit and every value is named at the tree, and the acceptance is a command with an expected output — not "decide the shape, then do it". | Read the row's own text; a row that names no file, or whose acceptance reads "cannot be pre-filled", fails here. |
| **A2** | **No live lane holds a file the row EDITS.** Citing a held file is permitted; editing one is not. | `git status --porcelain` over the clones that exist **at dispatch** — never a roster's prose (handoff §5.3). |
| **A3** | **No open decision.** The design shape is settled and no seat ruling is owed. | A row whose only blocker is one sentence of seat assent is listed in §9.7's "cheapest seat acts" instead. |
| **A4** | **Dependencies landed.** | `git log` at the tree, not a plan's status column. |
| **A5** | **The lane can execute its own proof** — Node in its own clone, or the named Edge leg under the slot lock. Network fetches at probe time and third-party builds fail here. | The proof shape column in §9.4/§9.5. |
| **A6** | **Size** — one lane sitting, with the estimate stated. | The estimate column; XL architecture rows fail. |

**Gemini.** The ruling gives Gemini "bounded docs and mechanical work under thirty minutes, every value specified, no build, no browser" — which is the tracked profile of §2.1 unchanged, plus the thirty-minute cap of `R-HANDOFF-1`. Two further filters did real work in this pass: a row is **not** Gemini if any acceptance step requires **authoring** something the row does not supply (this is what moved `C13-N58` to Astra — its acceptance runs a `shader-identity.mjs` whose body no document contains), and a row is not Gemini if its STOP condition asks for triage of a failing spec. Two rows, 45 minutes total. That is the whole Gemini claim in this table and it is a property of the work, not a judgement about Gemini: every other dispatchable row is spec code, tools code, engine/shader work, a measurement needing triage, or a browser leg — all excluded by the tracked Gemini profile and by R-2026-09-10-4. Both rows are byte-transcription with every value pinned, which is the shape Gemini is measured strongest at (65/65 ids set-identical, 12/12 rulings byte-verbatim).

### 9.3 What moved, and the counts

156 distinct rows across the four groups (166 entries, 10 of them duplicate ids: the nine C13-42*/C13-49-slice rows shared by G1 and G3, and DX-WAVE-END-GATE-DECOMPOSITION shared by G3 and G4). waitsForSeat counts rows whose START is blocked by a frozen lane's held file, an owed seat ruling, an unbuilt dependency inside the seat's own programme, or a seat act. It is false for the 21 solo rows and for the 24 rows that are done, closed, duplicate views of another id, header rows, or ids that do not exist at the tree. movedSince1482 = the nine rows added to the dispatchable set since the tracked roster of Batch 1482; none was removed from it.

**The nine rows added to the dispatchable set since the tracked roster of Batch 1482** are `C13-N07a`, `C13-42f`, `C13-42g`, `DX-89`, `DX-90`, `DX-91`, `DX-95`, `DX-WAVE-END-GATE-DECOMPOSITION` and `NEW-C13-42A-3-ITEM-6-BRANCH-ASSERTION` — seven freed by the Batch 1485 and 1486 landings and by the Edge slot falling free, two by the ruling itself. `DX-83` and `DX-84` are **restored** to the dispatchable set: the refuters' demotions rested on baselines measured at HEAD for rows pinned by `R-HANDOFF-5` to `9f3723b0b3`, which is a transcriber's standard applied to the wrong base (problem P-05).

### 9.6 Cross-row problems, and their resolutions

Eleven findings that belong to no single row. Three of them are the reason this section exists at all: **P-01** (the old Astra clone must never be re-used), **P-06** (the Edge-slot lock is per repository root, not per machine) and **P-05** (the demotions of `DX-83` and `DX-84` were measured at the wrong base). All three are restated for a stranger in [`SOLO_WORKER_HANDOFF_2026-09-13.md`](SOLO_WORKER_HANDOFF_2026-09-13.md) §7 and §8.

#### P-01 — F:/Dev/GH/cesium-astra-20260910 must NOT be Astra's clone — it carries 252 dirty paths, four of them this roster's own files

**Severity:** BLOCKING for the first dispatch

**Evidence.**

- measured 2026-09-13 at 0e4b898129: git -C /f/Dev/GH/cesium-astra-20260910 status --porcelain → 252 entries, HEAD 9fb5e65622 (Batch 1450, 36 batches behind)
- among them: CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, package.json, Tools/visual-regression/lib/probe-edge-slot.mjs (the lock this ruling names), Tools/lib/png-decode.mjs, Tools/visual-regression/probe-fleet-contract.spec.mjs, Tools/visual-regression/cloud-observability-counters.spec.mjs and cloud-probe-harness.spec.mjs (C13-N08b-A4's and L1's files), migration_doc/QUEUE_2026-08-29_RESEARCH_DISPATCH.md (+48/−0 — DX-83's only owned file), migration_doc/CAMPAIGN_STATE.md, Tools/verify-landing-compliance.mjs (maintainer-held)

**Resolution.** The seat provisions a FRESH clone (`node Tools/provision-worker-clone.mjs <clone>`, exit 0 required) at a stated base and names it in the dispatch message and in every packet. 'Astra runs alone in a named clone' must never be read as re-using this directory. Decide separately whether the 252 paths are harvested or discarded; that is a seat act, not the lane's.

#### P-02 — Five ASTRA-SOLO rows collide on two files and one package.json neighbourhood — they are a sequence, never concurrent lanes

**Severity:** BLOCKING if two lanes run

**Evidence.**

- Tools/visual-regression/sandcastle-smoke.mjs (780 lines, measured) is edited by DX-91 (:718, the report filename — verbatim at the tree), DX-90 (the per-demo watchdog) and DX-95 (the same per-demo verdict function DX-90 touches)
- package.json: DX-90/DX-91/DX-95 each append to :167 (test-sandcastle) while DX-84 appends to :170 (test-tools-lib) — three lines apart, so a default -U3 hunk for :170 spans :167-:173
- Tools/wave-end-gate.mjs is DX-WAVE-END-GATE-DECOMPOSITION's and would also be C13-N48-BASELINE-PROMOTION-BINDABILITY's if that row is ruled (a)

**Resolution.** One Astra lane takes all of DX-91 → DX-90 → DX-95 in that order (DX-91 is XS and its rename is what the other two's evidence lands in). DX-84 lands in a separate sitting from all three, or one lane unions the package.json edit. Never two concurrent Astra lanes on this set — one defect, one owner applies to files, not only to rows.

#### P-03 — C13-N58 measures the two specs DX-89 edits — an ordering the classifiers and both refuters missed

**Severity:** NON-BLOCKING but silently invalidating

**Evidence.**

- C13-N58's acceptance is `node --test webgpu-cloud-godray-current-mask-order.spec.mjs` → 24/24/0 and `cloud-primary-ray.spec.mjs` → 5/5/0
- DX-89's chosen arm edits exactly those two files to add a build preflight (skip-with-reason), which changes both counts
- both specs are named in package.json:207 test-cloud-c13 (measured)

**Resolution.** Run C13-N58 first and bank its receipts; then DX-89. If DX-89 lands first, C13-N58's expected values must be re-derived before dispatch.

#### P-04 — package.json is a contended file with four distinct keys in play, and the contention has moved twice since the inputs were written

**Severity:** NON-BLOCKING, seat unions at landing

**Evidence.**

- measured at 0e4b898129 across the surviving clones: ulmo and manwe both hold :207 (test-cloud-c13); osse holds :209 (test-visual-regression-node); bandobras holds no package.json; Huan's and Curumo's clones are gone, so :209's second holder and Oromë's :167 are both discharged
- :167 (test-sandcastle), :168 (test-landing-rules), :170 (test-tools-lib), :205 (test-visual-probe-contracts) and :208 (test-cloud-c13-quarantine) are all free
- the six quarantine rows' own runner home (:208) is held by nobody — they are uncontended

#### P-05 — Runner-baseline contradictions: five different pinned counts in this set are wrong against HEAD, and two of them are right against the pinned base

**Severity:** BLOCKING for a byte-transcriber, a re-measure for a lead

**Evidence.**

- quarantine (six rows): 150/135/15 — I re-ran it at 0e4b898129 and it still holds, so R-HANDOFF-6's sibling clause is the only arithmetic issue
- test-tools-lib (DX-84): row pins 96/96/0 → 108/108/0; measured 103/103/0 at HEAD, so the target is 115/115/0. 96/96/0 is correct at 9f3723b0b3
- test-c13-42 (C13-42g): row rests on 10 tests / 115.6-136 s; measured 16/16/0 in 62.2 s
- DX-83: row pins 19 and 3; measured 21/22 and 6 at HEAD, 19/20 and 3 at 9f3723b0b3
- C13-N59: row pins 449 lines and 325/302; measured 451 and 327/306 at HEAD, 449 at 9f3723b0b3
- spec-runner-census (DX-94): row pins 375/166/209; measured 377/168/209 — the ratchet bound orphaned <= 209 is unchanged and would land green

**Resolution.** Split by worker class, which is what both refuters failed to do. For GEMINI rows (DX-83, C13-N59) pin the clone to 9f3723b0b3 per R-HANDOFF-5 — every value then reads as written — or paste the refreshed values from this file. For ASTRA rows the stale count is a re-measure, not a stop: the dispatch says 'measure your own base, record both numbers, continue', exactly as R-HANDOFF-6 already says for the six quarantine rows. Demoting an Astra row over a moved runner count applies a transcriber's standard to a lead.

#### P-06 — The Edge slot lock is PER-REPOSITORY-ROOT, not per machine — the ruling's premise does not hold as written

**Severity:** BLOCKING for any Astra Edge leg

**Evidence.**

- Tools/visual-regression/lib/probe-edge-slot.mjs:33-34 — DEFAULT_EDGE_SLOT_LOCK_PATH is the RELATIVE path 'Tools/visual-regression/output/.edge-slot.lock'
- Tools/visual-regression/lib/probe-runtime.mjs:940 and :955 — acquireEdgeSlot({ lockPath: path.join(repositoryRoot, DEFAULT_EDGE_SLOT_LOCK_PATH) }), and repositoryRoot defaults to process.cwd() (:892-893) with a --repository-root override
- so a lock taken inside Astra's clone excludes nothing running in the seat tree or in another clone
- EDGE_SLOT_STALE_AFTER_MS = 2 h (:41); a stale or dead-pid holder is reclaimed (decideEdgeSlot, :69)
- measured: F:/Dev/GH/cesium-webgpu/Tools/visual-regression/output/.edge-slot.lock does not exist — the slot is free
- measured netstat: THREE foreign node servers, not two — PID 6904 (--port 8080 --sandcastlePort 8082 --serve-built), PID 17856 (--port 8094 --serve-built), PID 18208 (--port 8095 --sandcastlePort 8083 --serve-built)

**Resolution.** See edgeSlotProtocol: Astra takes the SEAT TREE's lock file explicitly as the machine lock for the whole leg, in addition to whatever its own clone's runtime takes, and releases it by token in a finally. And the brief's 'the only foreign servers are :8094 and :8095' is corrected to three processes holding six ports; Astra starts its own server on its own port from its own built clone and kills only PIDs it started.

#### P-07 — Anchor drift into the frozen lanes' files: six rows CITE files L3/L4/L5 hold, and one cites a file dirty in the old Astra clone

**Severity:** NON-BLOCKING — line numbers move, content does not

**Evidence.**

- C13-N08b-A1 cites ProceduralClouds.wgsl (L4, +219/−13); A3/A4/A5/B and C13-N08b-C cite WebGPUProceduralCloudRenderer.ts (L3 +106/−142, L5 two lines at :204-205)
- both refuters grepped all three lane diffs for each row's pinned symbols (coverageThreshold, smoothstep(1.0 -, resetCloudFrameCounters, culledFrames, temporalHistoryResetCount, applyEclipseCloudDimming, eclipseCloudFactor, reconstructionEnabled, attachmentRenderedThisFrame) and found NOTHING
- GODRAY-SUN-USABILITY-RANGE-CONTRACT-RED cites WebGPUGodRayEffect.ts at seven places; that file is +53/−8 in cesium-astra-20260910 (P-01)

**Resolution.** Every brief that cites a held file says: the citation is a READ, the edit is elsewhere, and the line numbers are re-derived in your own clone at the dispatch base before you touch anything. That is the whole cost — the refuters proved it is not rework. C13-N08b-B additionally must LEAVE F1a/F1b red, because F1a pins a shader L4 is rewriting.

#### P-08 — Four unresolved refutations that must be struck from any brief built on these inputs

**Severity:** BLOCKING for brief-writing (Principle 10)

**Evidence.**

- C13-N11 / C13-N17b: the G1 classifier swapped two line numbers and declared the plan's anchor false. Measured by the refuter: ProceduralClouds.wgsl:2537 IS the multiScatterLight call with the literal 0.5, and :1940 is `morphologyCoordinate: vec3<f32>,`. The plan's C13-N11 anchor resolves exactly; C13-N17b's finding (CLOUD_SHADOW_FOOTPRINT_M is in the renderer, not any .wgsl) stands on its own
- C13-N37: the plan's premise at :447 is measured-false — the epsilon debounce shipped at Batch 743. The row is a ledger close plus a plan correction, not work
- C13-N24: the classifier's 'C13-N24a is ASTRA-SOLO today at ~8 h' fails — `git ls-files | grep -ci grib` = 0, so the offline arm has nothing to decode, and its values' source (Tilion's seal) is untracked until C13-N59 lands
- duplicate ids: DX-92 ≡ GODRAY-…-RED, NEW-LANDING-GATE-RUNNER-RESOLVER ≡ DX-93, NEW-SPEC-HOME-RATCHET ≡ DX-94, NEW-TEST-CLOUD-C13-BUILD-PREFLIGHT ≡ DX-89, C13-42g ≡ DX-81, and DX-WAVE-END-GATE-DECOMPOSITION appears in two of the four groups

**Resolution.** The assembler carries the corrected facts, not the classifier's. C13-N11 and C13-N10 are L3/L4 lane content and are never re-dispatched. C13-N37 goes to the seat as a close. C13-N24a is not filed until C13-N59 lands or a fetch is ruled. Every duplicate id is dispatched under its canonical id only, once.

#### P-09 — R-2026-09-13-8 has no predecessors and no home file at the tree

**Severity:** NON-BLOCKING, seat bookkeeping

**Evidence.**

- measured: `grep -rn 'R-2026-09-13-[0-9]' migration_doc/*.md` returns NOTHING at 0e4b898129
- the dated rulings files run …2026-09-10, -11, -12; there is no MAINTAINER_RULINGS_2026-09-13.md
- the 2026-09-13 seat rulings that exist were appended to the 2026-09-12 file as numbered items 10-13, and item 13 points at SOLO_WORKER_HANDOFF §2.3 as R-HANDOFF-*'s authority

**Resolution.** The seat creates migration_doc/MAINTAINER_RULINGS_2026-09-13.md with rulingText below, and either mints -1..-7 for the day's other rulings or records in one sentence why the sequence starts at -8 (add-only numbering, ORCHESTRATION_HANDBOOK §4). Add the file to migration_doc/README.md in the same batch or verify-readme-index goes red.

#### P-10 — The ruling makes Astra its own reviewer, which charter §4.6 forbids in general — the self-review bar must replace the missing independence structurally

**Severity:** DESIGN — it is what astraSelfReviewBar exists for

**Evidence.**

- WORKER_ISOLATION_AND_BRANCH_HANDOFF.md §8a R6 — 'Charter §4.6 forbids the author approving its own work, and a handoff report is the author's account'
- Principle 10 — 'A spec written from the same brief as the fix is not an independent check. It inherits the brief's errors.'
- the tracked Astra profile records an unsupervised window producing 72 modified / 34 deleted / 146 untracked files with an empty index, a green spec taken to 0/5 fleet-wide, 232 specs left with runner NONE, and a breached archival hold

**Resolution.** The maintainer's ruling governs, so the substitute is mechanical rather than social: premises re-derived from the tree and never from the brief; every spec proving OUTPUT, not the instruction; inertness mutants that make the change UNREACHABLE (`if (false && …)`) rather than absent; and a final adversarial pass in which the lane argues against its own premises in writing. The seat still reviews at landing — that review is the only remaining independence and must not be skipped because the lane self-reviewed.

#### P-11 — Edge-slot contention among the Astra rows themselves — four rows need the browser and there is one slot

**Severity:** NON-BLOCKING with sequencing; none of it is the seat's now

**Evidence.**

- Edge-needing ASTRA-SOLO rows: C13-N07a (two captures plus baseline promotion), C13-42f (one capture), DX-90 (one live leg), DX-95 (a two-settle live run)
- no seat executor holds the slot and the lock file does not exist (measured)
- R-2026-09-12-7's ordering (C13-41 first, then L6→L3→L4→L5→L7) is spent — the discriminator has already returned once and the seat has wound down

**Resolution.** One Edge leg at a time in the order given by `sequencing`; each leg banks under Tools/visual-regression/output/<probe>/ in the probe's own layout and repatriates before the clone is reset.


### 9.7 Sequencing

**One Astra lane at a time in a single fresh clone. The order below honours (a) chains, (b) shared edit files, (c) the single Edge slot, and (d) putting the two rows whose value is highest and whose blockers just cleared before the long tail. Freeze and packet each row before starting the next; the seat may land them in batches.**

| Step | Rows | Why here |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `C13-N58` | Node-only, 45 min, and it MUST precede DX-89, which edits the two specs it measures (P-03). It also proves the fresh clone works before anything expensive runs in it. |
| 2 | `C13-N08b-A1`, `C13-N08b-A3`, `C13-N08b-A5`, `C13-N08b-A2`, `C13-N08b-A4`, `C13-N08b-B` | All six in ONE clone in this exact order, which makes the seven rows' contradictory quarantine totals a non-issue: 135 → 136 → 139 → 140 → 141 → 143 → 147, finishing 150/147/3 with F1a, F1b and S1 (the demoted C13-N08b-C) red by design. B last — it is the only one whose arithmetic assumes no sibling landed. Baseline 150/135/15 re-measured by me at 0e4b898129. |
| 3 | `C13-42f` | First Edge leg. Highest value in the table: it is the gate on C13-N47 (CHARACTERIZATION_THRESHOLDS is still null) and on C13-45, and its code half already landed, so the leg is short. |
| 4 | `C13-N07a` | Second Edge leg, and the best-specified unlanded row anywhere in this set — a tracked five-step recipe, two scenes, two baselines, and an acceptance that is the standing gate's own exit code. Not concurrent with step 3: one slot. |
| 5 | `C13-42g` | After C13-42f's leg, because it decimates the grid that leg exercises. Its first act is a re-measure: 16/16/0 in 62.2 s at HEAD, against the row's 10 tests / 136 s. It may close as overtaken. |
| 6 | `GODRAY-SUN-USABILITY-RANGE-CONTRACT-RED` | Node-only, one file, the most completely specified row in the set — six exact-match chunk edits, ten numbered acceptance commands, and a baseline I reproduced by execution (19/16/3, exactly B4/C2/C3). Dispatch DX-92 never: same defect, canonical id only. |
| 7 | `NEW-C13-42A-3-ITEM-6-BRANCH-ASSERTION` | Node-only, newly freed by Batch 1485, runner home free at package.json:205. Brief it 'assert the branch condition', never 'assert these four paths'. |
| 8 | `DX-82` | Node-only, two owned files, spec already homed at :168; anchors re-verified at the current tip (provisionNodeModulesJunctions at :605). Fold DX-85's residual in as a fourth change. |

| 10 | `DX-91`, `DX-90`, `DX-95` | All three in one lane in this order — they share sandcastle-smoke.mjs and DX-90/DX-95 rewrite the same verdict function. DX-91 is XS and its report rename is what the other two's evidence lands in. DX-90 and DX-95 each carry an Edge leg: one at a time. DX-95's brief must bound the blast radius — a third verdict changes report-<renderer>.json for the wave-end gate's receipt reader, and wave-end-gate.spec.mjs must stay 36/36/0. |

**Gemini order.** DX-83 (15 min) → C13-N59 (30 min). Both are pinned-base docs rows and touch nothing any Astra row edits, so they may run in parallel with the Astra lane — but not with each other, since the maintainer runs Gemini personally and R-HANDOFF-1 forbids idle waits.

**The cheapest seat acts that grow this list.** Each is one sentence of adjudication that converts a NOT-SOLO row into an Astra row:

- one sentence picking (a) or (b) on C13-N48-BASELINE-PROMOTION-BINDABILITY → ASTRA-SOLO (both files unheld, Node-only)
- one public compatibility ruling on erosionStrength → C13-16-EROSION-STRENGTH-RANGE-CONTRACT becomes an S-sized Astra row
- one sentence naming which of O3/O4/O6/O7 gate → C13-N04b becomes ASTRA-SOLO and feeds C13-N47
- one sentence ruling the declaration shape → C13-N34-GODRAY-CLOUD-AWARE-SCENE-PROPERTY becomes ASTRA-SOLO
- naming C13-01's and C13-02's residuals, or closing both rows

### 9.8 What this pass deliberately did not carry

1. **Duplicate ids are collapsed, and each is dispatched under its canonical id only, once.** The four group passes returned **166** entries for **156** distinct ids. The ten duplicates are the nine `C13-42*`/`C13-49-slice` rows shared by G1 and G3, and `DX-WAVE-END-GATE-DECOMPOSITION`, shared by G3 and G4. Separately, five ids in the inputs are **second views of a row that already has an id** and must never be dispatched twice: `DX-92` ≡ `GODRAY-SUN-USABILITY-RANGE-CONTRACT-RED`, `NEW-LANDING-GATE-RUNNER-RESOLVER` ≡ `DX-93`, `NEW-SPEC-HOME-RATCHET` ≡ `DX-94`, `NEW-TEST-CLOUD-C13-BUILD-PREFLIGHT` ≡ `DX-89`, and `C13-42g` ≡ `DX-81`.
2. **Four classifier findings the refuters overturned**, which no brief built on these inputs may repeat (problem P-08): the `C13-N11`/`C13-N17b` line-number swap — `ProceduralClouds.wgsl:2537` **is** the `multiScatterLight` call with the literal `0.5`, and `:1940` is `morphologyCoordinate: vec3<f32>,`, so the plan's `C13-N11` anchor resolves exactly; `C13-N37`, whose premise is measured-false because the epsilon debounce shipped at Batch 743, making it a ledger close plus a plan correction rather than work; and `C13-N24a`, which cannot be filed until `C13-N59` lands or a fetch is ruled, because `git ls-files | grep -ci grib` = 0.
3. **No row's wording was rewritten.** §3's row text remains the authority for what each row says; this section changes only the **class**, the **worker** and the **sequence**.
4. **Nothing was moved, repointed or archived**, and `MAINTAINER_RULINGS` was not edited — the record round writes `R-2026-09-13-8` into its own dated file (P-09).
